\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\bigl\|Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\bigl({\rm D}^{k_{1}}P_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}{\scriptstyle\ldots})-{\rm D}^{k_{1}}P_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}{\scriptstyle\ldots})\bigr)\,Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})\,\ldots\,{\rm D}^{k_{n_{*}}}P_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}{\scriptstyle\ldots})\,Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})\bigr\|^{\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{L}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\ldots

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\bigl\|Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,{\rm D}^{k_{1}}P_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}{\scriptstyle\ldots})\,Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\ldots\,\bigl({\rm D}^{k_{n_{*}}}P_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}{\scriptstyle\ldots})-{\rm D}^{k_{n_{*}}}P_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}{\scriptstyle\ldots})\bigr)\,Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})\bigr\|^{\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{L}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\bigl\|Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,{\rm D}^{k_{1}}P_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}{\scriptstyle\ldots})\,Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\ldots\,{\rm D}^{k_{n_{*}}}P_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}{\scriptstyle\ldots})\,\bigl(Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})-Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\bigr)\bigr\|^{\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{L}(\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}\Bigl)

using (
34
) with
C_{Q}\geq 1
and (
72
) with

\|\underline{\gamma}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}=\max_{1\leq i\leq k}\|\gamma_{i}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\textstyle\sum_{*}\Bigl(\bigl\|Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})-Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\bigr\|^{\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{L}(\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}\widetilde{C}^{k_{1}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\bigl(\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\bigr)^{k_{1}}\,C_{Q}\;\ldots\;\widetilde{C}^{k_{n_{*}}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\bigl(\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\bigr)^{k_{n_{*}}}\,C_{Q}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+C_{Q}\bigl\|{\rm D}^{k_{1}}P_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}{\scriptstyle\ldots})-{\rm D}^{k_{1}}P_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}{\scriptstyle\ldots})\bigr\|^{\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{L}(\overline{W}_{\epsilon},\overline{V}_{\epsilon})}C_{Q}\;\ldots\;\widetilde{C}^{k_{n_{*}}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\bigl(\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\bigr)^{k_{n_{*}}}\,C_{Q}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\ldots

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+C_{Q}\,\widetilde{C}^{k_{1}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\bigl(\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\bigr)^{k_{1}}\,C_{Q}\;\ldots\;\bigl\|{\rm D}^{k_{n_{*}}}P_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}{\scriptstyle\ldots})-{\rm D}^{k_{n_{*}}}P_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}{\scriptstyle\ldots})\bigr\|^{\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{L}(\overline{W}_{\epsilon},\overline{V}_{\epsilon})}C_{Q}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+C_{Q}\,\widetilde{C}^{k_{1}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\bigl(\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\bigr)^{k_{1}}\,C_{Q}\;\ldots\;\widetilde{C}^{k_{n_{*}}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\bigl(\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\bigr)^{k_{n_{*}}}\bigl\|Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})-Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\bigr\|^{\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{L}(\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}\Bigl)

using (
35
) and (
74
) with

M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})=\max\{1,\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty},\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\}

\displaystyle\leq\textstyle\sum_{*}\Bigl((n_{*}+1)(C_{Q})^{n_{*}}\widetilde{C}^{k_{1}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\;\ldots\;\widetilde{C}^{k_{n_{*}}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\,(C_{Q})^{2}\,c^{1}_{\mathcal{F}}(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon})\,M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k_{1}+\ldots+k_{n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+(C_{Q})^{n_{*}+1}\bigl(c^{k_{1}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon})+k_{1}\,\widetilde{C}^{k_{1}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\bigr)M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k_{1}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\cdot\widetilde{C}^{k_{2}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k_{2}}\;\ldots\;\widetilde{C}^{k_{n_{*}}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k_{n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\ldots

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+(C_{Q})^{n_{*}+1}\widetilde{C}^{k_{1}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k_{1}}\;\ldots\;\widetilde{C}^{k_{n_{*}-1}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k_{n_{*}-1}}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 10.00002pt\cdot\bigl(c^{k_{n_{*}}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon})+k_{n_{*}}\,\widetilde{C}^{k_{n_{*}}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon}\bigr)M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k_{n_{*}}}\Bigr)

using
C_{Q}\geq 1
and
\widetilde{C}^{k_{*}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\geq C^{k_{*}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\geq 1

\displaystyle\leq\textstyle\sum_{*}(C_{Q})^{n_{*}+1}\widetilde{C}^{k_{1}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}{\scriptstyle\ldots}\;\widetilde{C}^{k_{n_{*}}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k}\Bigl((n_{*}+1)\,c^{1}_{\mathcal{F}}(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon})+k\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt+c^{k_{1}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon})+\ldots+c^{k_{n_{*}}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon})\Bigr)

using the constant
C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}=(C_{Q})^{k+1}\bigl(\textstyle\sum_{*}\widetilde{C}^{k_{1}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\,\ldots\,\widetilde{C}^{k_{n_{*}}+1}_{\mathcal{F}}\bigr)
from (
76
)

\displaystyle\leq C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\,M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k}\Bigl((k+1)\,c^{1}_{\mathcal{F}}(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon})+k\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt+k\max\{c^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}),\ldots,c^{k+1}_{\mathcal{F}}(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon})\}\Bigr)

and hence

(77)

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{1},\ldots,\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{k})-{\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{1},\ldots,\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{k})\bigr\|^{\scriptscriptstyle\mathcal{L}(\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\leq C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\bigl(c^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)+\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon}\bigr)\,M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k}

with the function
c^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}:[0,\infty)\to[0,\infty)
,

c^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}(x):=(k+1)\,c^{1}_{\mathcal{F}}(x)+k\max\{c^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}(x),\ldots,c^{k+1}_{\mathcal{F}}(x)\}
which inherits monotonicity, continuity, and the value
c^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}(0)=0
from its constituents.

The notation
\|\underline{\gamma}\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}
in (
73
) and
M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})
for tuples in
\Gamma^{k}
in (
75
) will in the following be applied to tuples
(\gamma_{*},\ldots,\gamma_{*})
arising as subsets of vector entries of higher tangent vectors, when it is bounded by the fiber norm in (
4
). That is, for
\underline{\gamma},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}
we have

\|(\gamma_{*},\ldots,\gamma_{*})\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\;=\;\max_{*}\|\gamma_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\;\leq\;\max_{1\leq i\leq N_{\ell-1}}\|\gamma_{i}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\;\leq\;\|\underline{\gamma}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}\Gamma}_{\epsilon}

and thus

\displaystyle M_{\epsilon}((\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}),(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}))

\displaystyle=\max\{1,\|(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*})\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty},\|(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*})\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\}

\displaystyle\leq\max\{1,\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}\Gamma}_{\epsilon},\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\}\;=\;M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}),

where we extended the notation
M_{\epsilon}(\ldots)
to higher tangent vectors by

M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}):=\max\{1,\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}\Gamma}_{\epsilon},\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr\}.

Finally, recall that the differential
{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}
at a base point
(\underline{\mathfrak{c}},\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathfrak{C}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}V
is a Cartesian product of the identity on
{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathfrak{C}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}
and maps

{\rm D}\underline{w}(\underline{\gamma},\underline{v}):{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}V\to{\rm T}^{\ell-1}W
whose components are all sums of operators of the three types in (
70
).
We will now deduce uniform bounds and continuity estimates for these operators from (
76
) and (
77
).

Uniform Bounds on
O^{k}_{3,*}
:

For any
1\leq k\leq\ell-1
,
\epsilon\in\Delta
,
\underline{\gamma}=(\gamma_{0},\ldots)\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}
with
\|\gamma_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\leq\delta_{Q}
, and
\underline{v}\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}V
we obtain from (
76
)

\displaystyle\bigl\|O^{k}_{3,*}(\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|(\underline{\xi},\underline{y})\|\leq 1}\bigl\|{\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma_{0})(\gamma_{*}\ldots\gamma_{*})]y_{*}\bigr\|^{W}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\|y_{*}\|\leq 1}C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\bigl(\|(\gamma_{*}\ldots\gamma_{*})\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\bigr)^{k}\|y_{*}\|^{V}_{\epsilon}\;\leq\;C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\bigl(\|\underline{\gamma}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)^{k}.

Uniform Continuity of
O^{k}_{3,*}
:
For any
1\leq k\leq\ell-1
,
\epsilon\in\Delta
,
\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}=(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0},\ldots),\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}=(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0},\ldots)\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}
with
\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon},\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\leq\delta_{Q}
, and

\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}}\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}V
we obtain from (
77
)

\displaystyle\bigl\|O^{k}_{3,*}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}})-O^{k}_{3,*}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|(\underline{\xi},\underline{y})\|\leq 1}\bigl\|{\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*})]y_{*}-{\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*})]y_{*}\bigr\|^{W}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\|y_{*}\|\leq 1}\bigl\|\bigl({\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*})]-{\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*})]\bigr)y_{*}\bigr\|^{W}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\|y_{*}\|\leq 1}C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(c^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)+\|(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*})-(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*})\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon}\bigr)\,M_{\epsilon}((\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}),(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}))^{k}\|y_{*}\|^{V}_{\epsilon}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(c^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)+\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)\,M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k}.

Uniform Bounds on
O^{k}_{2,*}
:
For any
1\leq k\leq\ell-1
,
\epsilon\in\Delta
,
\underline{\gamma}=(\gamma_{0},\ldots)\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}
with
\|\gamma_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\leq\delta_{Q}
, and
\underline{v}\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}V
we obtain from (
76
)

\displaystyle\bigl\|O^{k}_{2,*}(\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|(\underline{\xi},\underline{y})\|\leq 1}\bigl\|{\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma_{0})(\gamma_{*}\ldots\xi_{*}\ldots\gamma_{*})]v_{*}\bigr\|^{W}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\|\xi_{*}\|\leq 1}C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\|\gamma_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\ldots\|\xi_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\ldots\|\gamma_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\|v_{*}\|^{V}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\bigl(\|(\gamma_{*}\ldots\gamma_{*})\|^{\Gamma^{k-1}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\bigr)^{k-1}\|v_{*}\|^{V}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\bigl(\|\underline{\gamma}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)^{k-1}\|\underline{v}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}V}_{\epsilon}.

Uniform Continuity of
O^{k}_{2,*}
:
For any
1\leq k\leq\ell-1
,
\epsilon\in\Delta
,
\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}=(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0},\ldots),\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}=(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0},\ldots)\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}
with
\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon},\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\leq\delta_{Q}
, and

\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}}\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}V
we obtain from (
76
) and (
77
)

\displaystyle\bigl\|O^{k}_{2,*}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}})-O^{k}_{2,*}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|(\underline{\xi},\underline{y})\|\leq 1}\bigl\|{\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\xi_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*})]v^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}-{\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\xi_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*})]v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\bigr\|^{W}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\|\xi_{*}\|\leq 1}\Bigl(\bigl\|{\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\xi_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*})](v^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}-v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*})\bigr\|^{W}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt+\bigl\|{\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\xi_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*})]v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}-{\rm D}^{k}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\xi_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*})]v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\bigr\|^{W}_{\epsilon}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\|\xi_{*}\|\leq 1}C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\Bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\ldots\|\xi_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\ldots\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\,\|v^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}-v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\|^{V}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt+\bigl(c^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)+\|(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\xi_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*})-(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\xi_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*})\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon}\bigr)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\cdot M_{\epsilon}((\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\xi_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}),(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\xi_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}))^{k}\|v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\|^{V}_{\epsilon}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\Bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\ldots\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\,\|v^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}-v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\|^{V}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt+\bigl(c^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)+\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell-1}_{\bullet}\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)\,M_{\epsilon}((\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}),(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}))^{k}\|v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\|^{V}_{\epsilon}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\bigl(c^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)+\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}\Gamma}_{\epsilon}+\|\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}V}_{\epsilon}\bigr)\,M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k}M_{\epsilon}(\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}}),

Uniform Bounds on
O^{k}_{1,*}
:

For any
1\leq k\leq\ell-1
,
\epsilon\in\Delta
,
\underline{\gamma}=(\gamma_{0},\ldots)\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}
with
\|\gamma_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\leq\delta_{Q}
, and
\underline{v}\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}V
we obtain from (
76
)

\displaystyle\bigl\|O^{k}_{1,*}(\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|(\underline{\xi},\underline{y})\|\leq 1}\bigl\|[{\rm D}^{k+1}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma_{0})(\gamma_{*}\ldots\gamma_{*},\xi_{0})]v_{*}\bigr\|^{W}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\|\xi_{0}\|\leq 1}C^{k+1}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\|\gamma_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\ldots\|\gamma_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\|\xi_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\|v_{*}\|^{V}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq C^{k+1}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\bigl(\|(\gamma_{*}\ldots\gamma_{*})\|^{\Gamma^{k}}_{\epsilon,\infty}\bigr)^{k}\|v_{*}\|^{V}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq C^{k+1}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\bigl(\|\underline{\gamma}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)^{k}\|\underline{v}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}V}_{\epsilon}.

Uniform Continuity of
O^{k}_{1,*}
:
For any
1\leq k\leq\ell-1
,
\epsilon\in\Delta
,
\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}=(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0},\ldots),\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}=(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0},\ldots)\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}
with
\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon},\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\leq\delta_{Q}
, and

\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}}\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}V
we obtain from (
76
) and (
77
)

\displaystyle\bigl\|O^{k}_{1,*}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}})-O^{k}_{1,*}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|(\underline{\xi},\underline{y})\|\leq 1}\bigl\|[{\rm D}^{k+1}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*},\xi_{0})]v^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}-[{\rm D}^{k+1}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*},\xi_{0})]v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\bigr\|^{W}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\|(\underline{\xi},\underline{y})\|\leq 1}\Bigl(\bigl\|[{\rm D}^{k+1}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*},\xi_{0})](v^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}-v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*})\bigr\|^{W}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt+\bigl\|[{\rm D}^{k+1}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*},\xi_{0})]v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}-[{\rm D}^{k+1}Q_{\epsilon}(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*},\xi_{0})]v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\bigr\|^{W}_{\epsilon}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\|\xi_{0}\|\leq 1}C^{k+1}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\Bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\ldots\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\|\xi_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\|v^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}-v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\|^{V}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt+\bigl(c^{k+1}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)+\|(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*},\xi_{0})-(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*},\xi_{0})\|^{\Gamma^{k+1}}_{\epsilon}\bigr)\,M_{\epsilon}((\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{*},\xi_{0}),(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\ldots\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*},\xi_{0}))^{k+1}\|v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{*}\|^{V}_{\epsilon}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq C^{k+1}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\bigl(c^{k+1}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)+\|\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}\Gamma}_{\epsilon}+\|\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}V}_{\epsilon}\bigr)\,M_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{k+1}M_{\epsilon}(\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}}).

Summarizing, the three types of operators
O^{k}_{*,*}
for
1\leq k\leq\ell-1
satisfy the following:

Uniform Bounds on
O^{k}_{*,*}
:

For any
\epsilon\in\Delta
,
(\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})=(\gamma_{0},\ldots)\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}V\simeq{\rm T}^{\ell-1}(\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}\times V)
with
\|\gamma_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\leq\delta_{Q}
we have

\displaystyle\bigl\|O^{k}_{*,*}(\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\max\bigl\{1,\|(\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}(\Gamma\times V)}_{\epsilon}\bigr\}^{k+1}

with
C^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}:=\max_{1\leq k\leq\ell}C^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}
.

Uniform Continuity of
O^{k}_{*,*}
:
For any
\epsilon\in\Delta
,
(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}})=(\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0},\ldots),(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}})=(\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0},\ldots)\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}V\simeq{\rm T}^{\ell-1}(\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}\times V)
with
\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon},\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\leq\delta_{Q}
we have

\displaystyle\bigl\|O^{k}_{*,*}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}})-O^{k}_{1,*}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\leq C^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\bigl(c^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)+\|(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}})-(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}})\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}(\Gamma\times V)}_{\epsilon}\bigr)\,M_{\epsilon}((\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}}),(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}}))^{k+2}

with the new function
c^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}:[0,\infty)\to[0,\infty)
given by

c^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}(x):=\max_{1\leq k\leq\ell}c^{k}_{{\rm D}Q}(x)
, which inherits monotonicity, continuity, and the value
c^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}(0)=0
from its constituents. .

Since these operators sum to the nontrivial components of
{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}
in (
70
), we now obtain:

Uniform Bounds on
{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}
:

For any
\epsilon\in\Delta
and

\underline{z}=(\underline{\mathfrak{c}},\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathfrak{C}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}V\simeq{\rm T}^{\ell-1}(\mathcal{V}_{W}\times V)
with
\|\gamma_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\leq\delta_{Q}
we have

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}(\underline{z})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}(\overline{W}_{\epsilon}\times\overline{V}_{\epsilon}),{\rm T}^{\ell-1}(\overline{W}_{\epsilon}\times\overline{W}_{\epsilon}))}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt=\sup_{\|(\underline{\mathfrak{c}}^{\prime},\underline{\xi},\underline{y})\|\leq 1}\bigl\|\bigl(\underline{\mathfrak{c}}^{\prime},\underline{\xi},{\rm D}\underline{w}(\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})(\underline{\xi},\underline{y})\bigr)\bigr\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell-1}(W\times W)}_{\epsilon}

(78)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt=\sup_{\|(\underline{\mathfrak{c}}^{\prime},\underline{\xi},\underline{y})\|\leq 1}\Bigl(\bigl\|\bigl(\underline{\mathfrak{c}}^{\prime},\underline{\xi}\bigr)\bigr\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell-1}W}_{\epsilon}+\textstyle\sum_{i=0}^{N_{\ell-1}}\bigl\|{\rm D}w_{i}(\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})(\underline{\xi},\underline{y})\bigr\|^{W}_{\epsilon}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\leq 1+\textstyle\sum_{i=0}^{N_{\ell-1}}\textstyle\sum_{*,*}\bigl\|O^{k_{*}}_{*,*}(\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\leq 1+\textstyle\sum_{i=0}^{N_{\ell-1}}\textstyle\sum_{*,*}C^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\max\bigl\{1,\|(\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}(\Gamma\times V)}_{\epsilon}\bigr\}^{k_{*}+1}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\leq 1+\bigl(\textstyle\sum_{i=0}^{N_{\ell-1}}\textstyle\sum_{*,*}C^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigr)\ \max\bigl\{1,\|(\underline{\gamma},\underline{v})\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}(\Gamma\times V)}_{\epsilon}\bigr\}^{k_{*}+1}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\leq C^{\ell}_{{\rm T}Q}\,\max\bigl\{1,\|\underline{z}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}(W\times V)}_{\epsilon}\bigr\}^{\ell}

with a new constant
C^{\ell}_{{\rm T}Q}:=1+\textstyle\sum_{i=0}^{N_{\ell-1}}\textstyle\sum_{*,*}C^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}
.

Uniform Continuity of
{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}
:
For any
\epsilon\in\Delta
and

\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{l}}=(\underline{\mathfrak{c}}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}}),\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{k}}=(\underline{\mathfrak{c}}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}})\in{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathfrak{C}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}V\simeq{\rm T}^{\ell-1}(\mathcal{V}_{W}\times V)
with
\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon},\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\leq\delta_{Q}
we have

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}(\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{l}})-{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}(\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{k}})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}(\overline{W}_{\epsilon}\times\overline{V}_{\epsilon}),{\rm T}^{\ell-1}(\overline{W}_{\epsilon}\times\overline{W}_{\epsilon}))}

\displaystyle=\bigl\|\bigl(0,{\rm D}\underline{w}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}})-{\rm D}\underline{w}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}})\bigr)\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}(\overline{W}_{\epsilon}\times\overline{V}_{\epsilon}),{\rm T}^{\ell-1}(\overline{W}_{\epsilon}\times\overline{W}_{\epsilon}))}

(79)

\displaystyle=\textstyle\sum_{i=0}^{N_{\ell-1}}\bigl\|{\rm D}w_{i}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}})-{\rm D}w_{i}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}

\displaystyle\leq\textstyle\sum_{i=0}^{N_{\ell-1}}\textstyle\sum_{*,*}\bigl\|O^{k_{*}}_{*,*}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}})-O^{k_{*}}_{*,*}(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}})\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\times{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\overline{V}_{\epsilon},\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}

\displaystyle\leq\textstyle\sum_{i=0}^{N_{\ell-1}}\textstyle\sum_{*,*}C^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}\,\bigl(c^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)+\|(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}})-(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}})\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}(\Gamma\times V)}_{\epsilon}\bigr)

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt\cdot M_{\epsilon}((\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{l}}),(\underline{\gamma}^{\mathfrak{k}},\underline{v}^{\mathfrak{k}}))^{k_{*}+2}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\ell}_{{\rm T}Q}\bigl(c^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)+\|\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}(W\times V)}_{\epsilon}\bigr)\,M(\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{k}})^{\ell+1}.

\displaystyle=C^{\ell}_{{\rm T}Q}\bigl(c^{\leq\ell}_{{\rm D}Q}\bigl(\|\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0}-\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{\Gamma}_{\epsilon}\bigr)+\|\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{l}}-\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}(W\times V)}_{\epsilon}\bigr)\,\max\{1,\|\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{l}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}(W\times V)}_{\epsilon},\|\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{k}}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell-1}(W\times V)}_{\epsilon}\bigr\}^{\ell+1}.

Note here that
\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0},\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}
are the second entries of the base points of

\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{l}},\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{k}}
, that is

\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{l}}=(\mathfrak{c}^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0},\gamma^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0},v^{\mathfrak{l}}_{0},\ldots)\hskip 10.00002pt\text{and}\hskip 10.00002pt\underline{z}^{\mathfrak{k}}=(\mathfrak{c}^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0},\gamma^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0},v^{\mathfrak{k}}_{0},\ldots).

Finally, we have all preparations in place to prove adiabatic
\mathcal{C}^{\ell+1}
regularity in two claims.

Claim: [Pointwise Continuity of
\mathbf{{\rm D}}{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon}
in
\mathbf{\Delta}
]

For any
\epsilon_{0}\in\Delta_{\sigma}
and
(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0},\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1})\in{\rm T}^{\ell+1}\mathcal{V}_{\mathfrak{K}}

\bigl\|{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}-{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}\bigr\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell}W}_{\epsilon}\underset{\epsilon\to\epsilon_{0}}{\longrightarrow}0.

To prove this claim first recall from Lemma
4.5
that for each
\epsilon\in\Delta_{\sigma}
– in the above notation – we have the identity
\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}\circ\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon}={\rm Id}_{{\rm T}\mathcal{V}_{W}}
. This yields

{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}\circ{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon}={\rm T}^{\ell-1}{\rm Id}_{{\rm T}\mathcal{V}_{W}}
where
{\rm T}^{\ell-1}{\rm Id}_{{\rm T}\mathcal{V}_{W}}
is the identity map on
{\rm T}^{\ell-1}{\rm T}\mathcal{V}_{W}={\rm T}^{\ell}\mathcal{V}_{W}
, whose differential is again the identity map on
{\rm T}^{\ell}\mathcal{V}_{W}
. The resulting identity

{\rm Id}_{{\rm T}^{\ell}\mathcal{V}_{W}}={\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\bigl(\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}\circ\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon}\bigr)(\underline{w}_{\epsilon})={\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}\bigl({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon}(\underline{w}_{\epsilon})\bigr)\circ{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon}(\underline{w}_{\epsilon})

holds for each base point
\underline{w}_{\epsilon}\in{\rm T}^{\ell}\mathcal{V}_{W}
on the tangent space
{\rm T}_{\underline{w}_{\epsilon}}{\rm T}^{\ell}\mathcal{V}_{W}
, which is canonically identified with
{\rm T}^{\ell}W
.
Hence for fixed
\epsilon_{0}\in\Delta_{\sigma}
and
(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0},\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1})\in{\rm T}^{\ell+1}\mathcal{V}_{\mathfrak{K}}
we can apply the identity with varying base points
\underline{w}_{\epsilon}:={\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})
to the fixed vector

{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}\in{\rm T}_{{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\mathfrak{k}_{0})}{\rm T}^{\ell}\mathcal{V}_{W}={\rm T}^{\ell}W

to obtain

\displaystyle{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}

\displaystyle={\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}\bigl({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon}({\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}))\bigr)\,{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon}({\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}))\,{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}

(80)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:Psigma}}{=}{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}\bigl({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{\sigma}_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\bigr)\,{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon}({\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}))\,{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}.

After these preparations we can now estimate for
\epsilon_{0}\in\Delta_{\sigma}
,
(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0},\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1})\in{\rm T}^{\ell+1}\mathcal{V}_{\mathfrak{K}}
, and
\Delta_{\sigma}\ni\epsilon\to\epsilon_{0}

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}-{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}\bigr\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell}W}_{\epsilon}

using (
65
) and (
80
)

\displaystyle=\bigl\|{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{\sigma}_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}))\bigl({\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{\sigma}_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}-{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon}({\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}))\,{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}\bigr)\bigr\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell}W}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\leq\bigl\|{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{Q}_{\epsilon}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{\sigma}_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}))\bigr\|^{\mathcal{L}({\rm T}^{\ell-1}(\overline{W}_{\epsilon}\times\overline{V}_{\epsilon}),{\rm T}^{\ell}\overline{W}_{\epsilon})}\Bigl(\bigl\|{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{\sigma}_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}-{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon}({\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}))\bigr){\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}\bigr\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell-1}(W\times V)}_{\epsilon}\Bigr)

using (
78
), the triangle inequality, and (
64
) at
\epsilon=\epsilon_{0}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\ell}_{{\rm T}Q}\,\max\{1,\|{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{\sigma}_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell-1}(W\times V)}_{\epsilon}\}^{\ell}\Bigl(\bigl\|{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{\sigma}_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}-{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{\sigma}_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}\bigr\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell-1}(W\times V)}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt+\bigl\|\bigl({\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon_{0}}({\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}))-{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon}({\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}))\bigr){\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\,\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1}\bigr\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell-1}(W\times V)}_{\epsilon}\Bigr)

using (
67
) and (
68
) for
(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0},\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{1})\simeq(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}^{\scriptscriptstyle ev},\underline{\mathfrak{k}}^{\scriptscriptstyle od})\in{\rm T}^{\ell}\mathcal{V}_{\mathfrak{K}}\times{\rm T}^{\ell}\mathfrak{K}

and (

) with notation (
81
), (

)

\displaystyle\leq C^{\ell}_{{\rm T}Q}\,\max\{1,\delta_{\sigma}+c^{\ell-1}_{\sigma}(\|\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell}\mathfrak{K}})\,b^{\ell-1}_{\sigma}(\|\mathfrak{k}_{0}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell}\mathfrak{K}})+\|\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0}\|^{{\rm T}_{\bullet}^{\ell}\mathfrak{K}}\}^{\ell}\Bigl(\bigl\|{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\sigma_{\epsilon}(\mathfrak{k}^{\scriptscriptstyle ev})-{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\mathfrak{k}^{\scriptscriptstyle ev})\bigr\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell-1}W}_{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 20.00003pt\hskip 20.00003pt+c^{\ell+1}_{{\rm T}\mathcal{F}}(\|\underline{\gamma}_{\epsilon}-\underline{\gamma}_{\epsilon_{0}}\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell}\Gamma}_{\epsilon})\,\|\underline{\xi}_{0}\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell}\Gamma}_{\epsilon}+\bigl\|{\rm T}^{\ell+1}\mathcal{F}_{\epsilon}(\underline{\gamma}_{\epsilon_{0}},\underline{\xi}_{0})-{\rm T}^{\ell+1}\mathcal{F}_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\gamma}_{\epsilon_{0}},\underline{\xi}_{0})\bigr\|^{{\rm T}^{\ell+1}\Omega}_{\epsilon}\Bigr)

using the convergence in (

) and (

)

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\underset{\epsilon\to\epsilon_{0}}{\longrightarrow}\hskip 10.00002pt0.

Here the last step requires an estimate for the maps

\widetilde{P}_{\epsilon}:\mathfrak{C}\times\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}\times\mathfrak{C}\times\Gamma\to\mathfrak{C}\times\mathcal{V}_{\Gamma}\times\mathfrak{K}\times\Omega

given by

These are independent from
\epsilon
in the first three factors, and in the last factor amount to the maps

Their higher tangent maps

.
Taking the differential of this identification then identifies

\epsilon
-independent map

.
Now the term to be estimated is a difference between the maps
{\rm D}{\rm T}^{\ell-1}\widetilde{P}
at

and
\epsilon
with base points

(81)

\underline{w}_{\epsilon_{0}}:=(\underline{\gamma}_{\epsilon_{0}},\underline{\mathfrak{c}}_{\epsilon_{0}}):={\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon_{0}}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})\hskip 10.00002pt\text{and}\hskip 10.00002pt\underline{w}_{\epsilon}:=(\underline{\gamma}_{\epsilon},\underline{\mathfrak{c}}_{\epsilon}):={\rm T}^{\ell}\sigma_{\epsilon}(\underline{\mathfrak{k}}_{0})

applied to the fixed vector
